864 entries, show-env-status to signatures. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **show-env-status**: Show mise environment status — tools, env vars, tasks, release readiness. TRIGGERS - mise status, mise env, repo status, environment check. ([RocketSurgeonsGuild/Testing](https://github.com/RocketSurgeonsGuild/Testing/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/show-env-status) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **show-every-cut**: Use before you shorten, summarize, condense, trim, paraphrase or rewrite text a person gave you (a message, notes, a document, a post, a quote), before you fit their text to a character or word limit, and when some of their passages must stay word for word. ([mike-bronner/workbench-core](https://github.com/mike-bronner/workbench-core/tree/HEAD/skills/show-every-cut))
- **show-exec-form**: Surfaces the EXEC_* marker lines emitted by the plugin-level SessionStart hooks, which run hook commands in EXEC FORM (args present). Used to test whether exec form resolves ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT} to a real path and exports it as an env var on Cowork, where shell form (§2.1/§2.2) failed both. ([kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin](https://github.com/kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/cowork-exec-form-probe/skills/show-exec-form))
- **show-expansion**: Surfaces the EXP_ marker lines that the plugin-level SessionStart hooks injected, so the top-level vs bash -c expansion behavior can be read off. ([kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin](https://github.com/kazukinagata/analyzing-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/cowork-expansion-probe/skills/show-expansion))
- **show-external-agents**: 打开外部 agent（kimi / cursor / codex / grok / opencode / pi / zcode）的实时日志面板。这是一个「只开面板」的技能——不查询、不读日志、不做分析。当用户说"打开外部 agent 面板 / 把面板打开 / 我想看外部 agent 的界面 / 打开日志面板 / 让我看看外部 agent"，或在输入区的技能（斜杠）菜单里选中本技能时使用。 ([sunny0826/dim-external-agents-extend](https://github.com/sunny0826/dim-external-agents-extend/tree/HEAD/skills/show-external-agents) · ★2)
- **show-fabric-inventory**: Searches and lists Fabric inventory — connections, ports, cloud routers, service tokens, route filters, route aggregations, time services. Use when a user asks to show, list, find, search, count, or filter by state, metro, bandwidth, type, or expiry — one type or broadly (e.g. what do I have running, show me everything). Never route these seven to a generic filter tool. For other types (streams, agents, service profiles, metros, metrics, cloud events) call create_filter. ([equinix/equinix-agent-plugins](https://github.com/equinix/equinix-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/fabric-agent-plugins/skills/show-fabric-inventory))

- **show-jira-issue**: Use this skill when the user asks about a specific Jira issue by key (e.g. PROJ-123, ENG-456) — for viewing the description, status, comments, transitions, or any other field. Trigger when the user says 'look up', 'check on', 'tell me about', 'what's on', or 'what is the status of' a key, or asks any direct question about an issue they reference. Do NOT trigger when an issue key appears incidentally inside other prose (commit messages, code review comments, release notes), where the user is talking about the issue rather than asking to fetch it. ([atomicinnovation/accelerator](https://github.com/atomicinnovation/accelerator/tree/HEAD/skills/integrations/jira/show-jira-issue) · ★33)
